# GUARDNET

**Complete: 16/16 helper regions have completed priced searches over ticks 25–43 with 9 wires.**
Current incumbent: v36j; tau = 3.798125294382715e-5; exact acceptance floor 0.5002.
64/64 primary solves finished pricing (73 including pilot/adaptive searches); 1450 distinct saved circuits priced; 2 gains have judge receipts.
Best feasible exact price: `cand/guardnet_best.qasm`, L=3.7979877231691608e-05, A=0.50054898251950053, J_tau=-6.8861130968691349e-10.

## Decisions and scope
- The corrected stop rule replaces the prior early stop: a finalist is retained and reported while coverage continues. Previous APROC recovery remains in the ledger but is worse than v36j.
- Reproduced v36j exactly before synthesis. Did not read or rerun BUNDLE res/b2.jsonl.
- Regions: one connected 9-wire window per non-data grid neighbour of D, including every neighbour of its central helper; explicit cells and data support are in regions.json.
- Each window spans all ticks 25–43. Joint CP-SAT selects disjoint nearest-neighbour CXs and movable helper preparations/readouts; the exterior and data one-qubit operations stay in place.
- Helper one-qubit event order is preserved, with all window ticks eligible subject to occupancy. Event-labelled coherent rows retain measurement/reset identities under retiming.
- Synthesis preserves APROC-style full coherent boundary; every emitted witness also passes the independent exact ideal accepted-state oracle. Every emitted candidate is priced at full noise.
- Grammar: baseline internal CX +8 (+10 for echo templates), seeded linear proposal objectives; up to 24 improving witnesses per solve (32 for echo templates), 180 solver seconds (240 echo). These finite searches are not exhaustive enumeration or a global optimality proof.
- Families: joint current baseline, joint q25-funded baseline, fixed-readout CX control, measured-to-reset templates, and q6 guard-to-X-echo templates. Funding sources are re-priced in their actual composition.
- Adaptive passes: exact-price linear feedback on h01/h02; cheaper q28-reset funding on h16/h19/h26/h28; identity-guard timing synthesis; exhaustive q0/q7 guard reassignment with fixed preparations and covered data slots.
- Winner: at tick35 replace CX6→0 and CX13→7 with CX1→0 and CX6→7; at tick36 replace CX6→0 with CX1→0. Same 357 CX, 47 ticks and detectors. No acceptance funding is needed.
- Measured interaction: tick35 alone costs +4.29973699289e-8 L (d4021449046c); tick36 alone +2.27595021126e-8 (9dc9f6c6e64d); together gain 1.37571213555e-9 (3f94de4d5d6b). See interaction.json and best_delta.json; both components would be rejected by single-edit greedy selection.
- On v36j, q25 echo deletion costs +4.56711316760e-9 L and buys +0.00497087259449 A. This replaces the older v36h estimate for decisions in this lane.
- Differences within max(5e-14, 10 times the evaluator kernel omission bound) are numerical ties, not verified gains; raw values remain in the table.
- Sustained compute: tom one parent + ten single-process workers; zebra up to two pool workers + parent and one separate worker. Transient collection/audit commands are separate from these worker counts. Thread caps are 1; writes stay in GUARDNET; no submission, git mutation, GPU or subagents.

## Verified versus inferred
Verified: exact v36i/v36j controls; saved QASM/evaluator hashes and full-circuit all-orders prices; emitted schedule occupancy/grid checks, coherent boundary equality and independent ideal-state oracle. Judge gains are recorded separately with 1e6-shot receipts.
Inferred only: proposal objectives correlate with protection quality. Search coverage is finite-grammar coverage, not proof that all circuits or all feasible schedules were exhausted. MC cannot resolve tiny exact gains.
Files: cand/ (verified handoffs), builds/ (all saved prices), results.json, regions.json, specs/, logs/, network.py, campaign.py, prepare_campaign.py, collect.py, lane-local sparse/.
